In SdSET, the one-loop power spectrum is given by several terms

\displaystyle\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(1)}

\displaystyle=H^{2}[aH]^{-2\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(1)}+H^{2}[aH]^{-2\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\delta\alpha^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 13.0pt+2\frac{c_{4,0}}{9}\frac{1}{3!}H^{2}[aH]^{-4\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}^{3}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(0)}+\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{{\rm IC}^{(1)}}\,,

(4.19)

where
c_{4,0}\sim\lambda
, and
{\underline{\varphi}}
is given in
eq.

4.7
. The subscript
(n)
labels the order in
\lambda
in which correlator is calculated and
\delta\alpha^{(m)}
is the contribution to the correlator from a shift in the value of
\alpha
at order
\lambda^{m}
.
The first term is the one-loop power spectrum in the EFT,

\displaystyle[aH]^{-2\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(1)}

\displaystyle=-[aH]^{-4\alpha}\frac{1}{2\nu}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}\bigg)\frac{\lambda}{4k^{3-2\alpha}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{1}{p^{3-2\alpha}}

\displaystyle\to-\frac{1}{6\pi^{2}}\frac{\lambda}{4k^{3}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{k}{[aH]}\bigg)\bigg(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{K}{[aH]}\bigg)\,.

We must also allow for the possibility that, due to matching, we will need to correct the value of
\alpha
from its free-field value. Substituting
\alpha\to\alpha+\delta\alpha^{(1)}
in
eq.

3.18a
and expanding to linear order, we have

H^{2}[aH]^{-2\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\delta\alpha^{(1)}}=\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle_{(0)}\bigg(1+2\delta\alpha^{(1)}\log\frac{k}{[aH]}\bigg)\,,

(4.21)

where have absorbed the
[aH]^{-2\delta\alpha}
into the definition of this term to derive the dimensionless argument of the log. From the EFT point of view,
\delta\alpha^{(1)}
is an unknown constant of order
\lambda
to be fixed by matching. Finally, we have the contribution to the
{\underline{\varphi}}
power spectrum from the field redefinition:

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{4,0}}{9}\frac{1}{3!}H^{2}[aH]^{-4\alpha}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}^{3}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(0)}\to\frac{c_{4,0}}{9}\frac{1}{2k^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{K}{[aH]}+\log 2\bigg)\,.

(4.22)

We emphasize that since the coefficient of
\varphi_{+}^{3}
in the definition of
{\underline{\varphi}}
is fixed by matching the superhorizon six-point function, there is no additional freedom within this term that can be used to match the 1-loop power spectrum.

The final term
\delta\big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC}
is time independent and is determined by matching the time-independent part of the UV calculation. Combining these results and using
c_{4,0}\to\lambda
, we find

\displaystyle H^{2}\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(1)}=

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}}{6\pi^{2}}\frac{\lambda}{4k^{3}}\bigg(\frac{1}{-2\alpha}+\log\frac{[aH]}{k}+\frac{2}{3}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{-2\alpha}+\log\frac{[aH]}{K}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\delta\alpha^{(1)}\frac{H^{2}}{k^{3}}\log\frac{k}{a}+\frac{\lambda}{9}\frac{1}{2k^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\log 2+\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{{\rm IC}^{(1)}}\ .

(4.23)

Comparing this result to
, we see that the time-dependence of the UV and EFT agree after matching the single log coefficient with

\delta\alpha^{(1)}=\frac{\lambda}{8\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3}\bigg(\gamma_{E}-\frac{7}{3}+\log 2-\frac{1}{2}\big(\log 4\pi-\gamma_{E}\big)\bigg)\,.

(4.24)

This is a non-trivial result as we only have a single free parameter to match the full time-dependence on the UV result. All the time-independent contributions can be matched by adjusting the initial conditions, IC
(1)
, which in this case is just a renormalization of the amplitude of the two point function, namely a shift in
C_{\alpha}
.

One-loop Trispectrum

Now we move on to the one-loop trispectrum, as illustrated in the right side of
fig.

2
. For our purposes here, it suffices to simply match the UV divergent terms which result in time dependent
\log[aH]
factors.
6
6

6

We remind the reader that the “UV divergences” that we are isolating here take their origins from the IR divergences of the full theory that are resummed by Stochastic Inflation. The expansions being done to simplify the integrals that appear on the UV theory side of this matching calculation amount to decomposing the correlator via the method of regions
[
58
,
59
]
.
This will result in a correction to the
c_{3,1}
Wilson coefficient in the EFT. While the initial conditions also receive corrections from matching the trispectrum, these would contribute to Stochastic Inflation beyond NNLO, and so we will not compute them here.

For convenience, we break the UV calculation into two terms

\displaystyle{\cal I}_{4}

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\phi^{(2)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\phi^{(1)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\phi^{(2)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{3}\big)\phi^{(1)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle+{\rm permutations}

(4.25a)

\displaystyle{\cal K}_{4}

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\phi^{(3)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\phi^{(1)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\phi^{(1)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{3}\big)\phi^{(1)}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle+{\rm permutations}\,,

(4.25b)

where

\displaystyle\phi^{(2)}({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},t)

\displaystyle=i\int^{t}{\rm d}t_{1}\Big[H_{\rm int}(t_{1}),\phi\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},t\big)\Big]

(4.26a)

\displaystyle\phi^{(3)}({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},t)

\displaystyle=-\bigg[\int^{t_{1}}{\rm d}t_{2}H_{\rm int}(t_{2}),\bigg[\int^{t}{\rm d}t_{1}H_{\rm int}(t_{1}),\phi({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},t)\bigg]\bigg]\,,

(4.26b)

where
H_{\rm int}
is given in
eq.

3.5
. In what follows, we will show that
{\cal I}_{4}
is UV finite, while
{\cal K}_{4}
is UV divergent. Then we will match this divergent contribution between the UV theory and the EFT to derive the one-loop correction to
c_{3,1}
.

We begin by evaluating
{\cal I}_{4}
. Expanding in the limit
p\gg k_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle{\cal I}_{4}=\frac{1}{k_{2}^{3}k_{4}^{3}}

\displaystyle\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}G\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1},\tau,\tau_{1}\big)\frac{(1-ip\tau_{1})^{2}}{p^{3}}e^{i2p\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{4}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}G\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{3},\tau,\tau_{2}\big)\frac{(1+ip\tau_{2})^{2}}{p^{3}}e^{-i2p\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle+{\rm permutations}\,.

(4.27)

The integrals over
\tau_{1}
and
\tau_{2}
are independent and we can evaluate them as usual. Expanding in
k_{i}\tau_{i}\ll 1
, we have

\displaystyle\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4}}G\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1},\tau,\tau_{1}\big)(1-ip\tau_{1})^{2}e^{i2p\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle\to-\frac{1}{3}\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4}}\big(\tau^{3}-\tau_{1}^{3}\big)(1-ip\tau_{1})^{2}e^{i2p\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-i)}{3}\left(\log p\tau-\frac{11}{12}\right)\,.

(4.28)

Putting this together yields

{\cal I}_{4}\simeq\frac{1}{k_{2}^{3}k_{4}^{3}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{1}{p^{6}}\left(\log p\tau-\frac{11}{12}\right)^{2}\,.

(4.29)

This integral will not lead to any UV divergences. Furthermore, this result is exact in
p\tau
since we have only expanded in
k/p
. Therefore all the subleading terms will be more UV convergent, and correspond to higher power corrections in the EFT. This shows that all we need to include when matching
c_{3,1}
is the correction due to
{\cal K}_{4}
.

The second term,
{\cal K}_{4}
, gives rise to more interesting UV behavior. Again expanding for large loop momentum
p\gg k_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle{\cal K}_{4}

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{H^{4}}{32(k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{d}p}{(2\pi)^{d}}\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{d+1}}G\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1},\tau,\tau_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 140.0pt\times 2{\rm Im}\int^{\tau_{1}}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{4}}\frac{1}{p^{6}}(1+ip\tau_{1})^{2}(1-ip\tau_{2})^{2}e^{-i2p(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt+{\rm permutations}\,.

(4.30)

We have expressed the commutator acting on the loop momenta in
\phi^{(3)}
in terms of the imaginary part to simplify the calculation. In this form, the time integrals can be evaluated exactly, giving

\displaystyle{\cal K}_{4}

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{H^{4}\lambda^{2}}{16(k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{d}p}{(2\pi)^{d}}\frac{1}{p^{3}}\bigg[\frac{10}{81}-\frac{1}{27}\gamma_{E}(2+3\gamma_{E})-\frac{5}{36}\pi^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt+\frac{1}{9}\left(\log\frac{2p}{[aH]}\right)^{2}+(1+3\gamma_{E})\log\frac{2p}{[aH]}+\frac{4}{9}\log\frac{k}{[aH]}\bigg]+{\rm permutations}

\displaystyle=\frac{H^{4}\lambda^{2}}{8(k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3}}\frac{1}{4\pi^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log\frac{k}{[aH]}+\log\frac{K^{2}}{4\pi}-\gamma_{E}\right)

(4.31)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\left[\frac{10}{81}-\frac{1}{27}\gamma_{E}(2+3\gamma_{E}+6\log 2)-\frac{5}{36}\pi^{2}+\frac{2\log 2+3\log^{2}2}{27}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+{O}\Big((\log[aH])^{2}\Big)+{\rm permutations}\,.

In the last line, we are only writing the terms that are linear in
\log[aH]
since this is what determines the contribution to the RG.

We can see the appearance of higher powers of log from the perturbative EFT contribution to the one-loop trispectrum

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}

\displaystyle\supset\frac{C_{\alpha}^{6}\sum_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{8(k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3-2\alpha}}\frac{1}{9}

\displaystyle\hskip 16.0pt\times\int^{\texttt{t}}{\rm d}\texttt{t}_{1}\int^{\texttt{t}_{1}}{\rm d}\texttt{t}_{2}\big([a(\texttt{t}_{1})H][a(\texttt{t}_{2})H]\big)^{-2\alpha}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{C_{\alpha}^{2}}{2p^{3-2\alpha}}

\displaystyle\to\frac{\sum_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{8(k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3-2\alpha}}\frac{1}{9}\,\frac{1}{2}\left(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}+\log[aH]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 16.0pt\times\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\left(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{K}{[aH]}+\log 2\right)\,,

(4.32)

where the compensating dimensionful factors that appear inside the
\log[aH]
term come from expanding the prefactor in the small
\alpha
limit.
One can check that this term matches the coefficient of the log
3
divergence of the UV calculation.

In order to match the linear log term, we need to keep track of the EFT field redefinition to order
\lambda^{2}
. Specifically, we need

{\underline{\varphi}}=H\left([aH]^{-\alpha}\varphi_{+}+[aH]^{-\beta}\varphi_{-}+\frac{\lambda}{9}\frac{1}{3!}[aH]^{-3\alpha}\varphi_{+}^{3}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{81}\frac{1}{3!}[aH]^{-5\alpha}\varphi_{+}^{5}\right)\ .

(4.33)

to remove the
\varphi_{+}^{6}
operator from the EFT potential. This
{O}\big(\lambda^{2}\big)
term contributes to the trispectrum at one loop:

\displaystyle\Big\langle{\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{3}\big){\underline{\varphi}}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}

\displaystyle\supset

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{(k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{3}}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{81}\frac{5!}{3!2}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{1}{p^{3-2\alpha}}\,,

(4.34)

which matches the leading UV term in
section

4.2
, namely the log term proportional to a factor of
10/81
. After matching this term, we see a fairly complicated expression remains for the linear log. This can be absorbed into the
c_{3,1}
Wilson coefficient in the potential using
eq.

3.23
, such that

c_{3,1}\to\lambda-\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{9}\gamma_{E}(2+3\gamma_{E}+6\log 2)+\frac{5}{12}\pi^{2}-\frac{2\log 2+3\log^{2}2}{9}\bigg)\,.

(4.35)

This is the one-loop matching correction to an EFT Wilson coefficient, in analogy with
eq.

4.24
above. A priori, one might think that we have to consider the divergence from the mixing of the operators
\varphi_{+}
and
\varphi_{+}^{3}
. In later sections, we will see that such a mixing is equivalent to a shift in the potential of the form in
eq.

4.35
, and therefore these two interpretations of the logarithmic growth are related to each other by a field redefinition.

4.3
Initial Conditions for Composite Operators

The above matching procedure is sufficient to regulate the correlation function of
\phi
and match
\varphi_{+}
correlators at separated points. Composite operators are defined when some of these operators are at coincident points. In Fourier space, this involves a convolution integral which can produce divergences that require renormalizing the composite operator itself.

Composite operators can be defined in this way purely within the EFT. Since we are regulating loops in the EFT with dynamical dim reg, this implies that we will need to know initial conditions for general
\alpha
. Fortunately, we will be interested in limits where the momenta are hierarchical (UV divergences of the momentum integrals), which simplifies the matching considerably.

We will start by taking the tree-level initial conditions for the trispectrum, and tying two of the legs together to form a
\varphi_{+}^{2}[{\vec{x}}=0]
composite operator:

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC}=\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC}\,,

(4.36)

where we are interested in determining the integrand of the right hand side in the limit
p\gg k_{i}
. For
\alpha=0
, we determined the initial conditions exactly in
eq.

4.13
. In order to regulate the UV divergence that comes from tying the two legs together, we want to evaluate this for general
\alpha
, while isolating the term of interest, which is proportional to
P_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})
, where
P_{+}
is defined by

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\Big\rangle=P_{+}(k)(2\pi)^{3}\delta\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}+{\!\!\vec{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{584904}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{731706}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{951907}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\,k$}}}}}}^{\hskip 0.8pt\prime}}}\big)\,,

(4.37)

so that
\big(P_{+}(k)\big)_{\text{tree}}=(2k^{3})^{-1}
. Since
k\ll p
, the initial conditions will arise at the horizon crossing of the modes carrying momentum
p
when the
k_{i}
-modes are superhorizon. As discussed in
section

4.2
, we therefore match using massive mode functions for only the
k_{i}
fields, where we can also Taylor expand in
k\tau\ll 1
. As a result, the initial conditions are given by

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\text{IC}^{(1)}}\simeq\lambda P_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\times\lim_{\tau_{0}\to 0}2\hskip 0.8pt{\rm Im}\int^{\tau_{0}}\frac{{\rm d}\tau}{(-H\tau)^{4}}[aH]^{-2\alpha}\frac{1}{4p^{2}}(1-ip\tau)^{2}(1+ip\tau_{0})^{2}e^{i2p(\tau-\tau_{0})}\,,

(4.38)

where
[aH]^{-2\alpha}=(-\tau)^{2\alpha}
. The RHS of this expression is the calculation in the UV theory with the appropriate choice of masses for the mode functions. We are implicitly evaluating the correlation function at a time
\tau_{0}
and extracting the
\tau_{0}
-independent piece in the
\tau_{0}\to 0
limit. Evaluating the integral, we find

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\lambda P_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})

\displaystyle\frac{(1+2\alpha)\Gamma[-1+2\alpha]\sin\big(\frac{\pi}{2}(1-2\alpha)\big)}{2^{2\alpha}p^{3+2\alpha}}\ .

(4.39)

Corrections to this result are suppressed by powers of
k_{i}/p
which will not contribute to the one-loop divergences that we will use to determine the RG for composite operator mixing below in
section

5.1
.

For two-loop divergences, one must determine the initial conditions (see
section

5.2
below):

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{3}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC^{(1)}}=\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p_{1}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}{}_{\alpha_{1}}\big({\vec{p}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}{}_{\alpha_{2}}\big({\vec{p}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}{}_{\alpha_{3}}\big(-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}-{\vec{p}}_{1}-{\vec{p}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{\rm IC^{(1)}}\,,\\

where we will take the limit
p_{i}\gg k
, such that
-\vec{k}-{\vec{p}}_{1}-{\vec{p}}_{2}\simeq-{\vec{p}}_{1}-{\vec{p}}_{2}
. We calculate this contribution for general
\alpha_{i}
by matching to the full theory:

\displaystyle\Big\langle\phi_{\alpha_{1}}\big({\vec{p}}_{1}\big)\phi_{\alpha_{2}}\big({\vec{p}}_{2}\big)\phi_{\alpha_{3}}\big({\vec{p}}_{3}\big)\phi\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}_{(1)}

\displaystyle=2\hskip 0.8pt{\rm Im}\bigg[u_{\nu_{1}}^{*}\big({\vec{p}}_{1},\tau\big)u_{\nu_{2}}^{*}\big({\vec{p}}_{2},\tau\big)u_{\nu_{3}}^{*}\big({\vec{p}}_{3},\tau\big)u_{3/2}^{*}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},\tau\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt\times\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4}}u_{\nu_{1}}\big({\vec{p}}_{1},\tau_{1}\big)u_{\nu_{2}}\big({\vec{p}}_{2},\tau_{1}\big)u_{\nu_{3}}\big({\vec{p}}_{3},\tau_{1}\big)u_{3/2}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},\tau_{1}\big)\bigg]\,,

where

u_{\nu}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}},\tau\big)=-i\hskip 0.8pte^{i\big(\nu+\frac{1}{2}\big)\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}H(-\tau)^{3/2}\text{H}_{\nu}^{(1)}(-k\tau)\,,

(4.41)

is the positive frequency mode for a field with
\alpha=\frac{3}{2}-\nu
.
Again, since the mode functions at horizon crossing behave as if they are effectively massless, this contribution can be determine using massless mode function of
p\tau_{1}
, but we must use the massive mode functions for
p\tau
. The result of integrating over
\tau_{1}
is

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{2}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{3}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\Big\rangle_{\rm IC^{(1)}}^{\prime}=\lambda\,\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{3}C_{\alpha_{i}}\hskip 0.8ptp^{\alpha_{i}}}{12\big(p_{1}p_{2}p_{3}\big)^{3}}\bigg(\sum_{i}\kappa_{i}\hskip 0.8ptp_{i}^{3}-p_{1}p_{2}p_{3}+\sum_{i\neq j}p_{i}^{2}p_{j}\bigg)\,,

(4.42)

where the correlators were matched at time
\tau
,

\kappa_{i}=\frac{1}{3}\left(-\frac{1}{2\alpha}+\gamma_{E}-2+\log\frac{p_{t}}{p_{i}}\right)\ ,

(4.43)

and
p_{t}=p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}
.

t_{1}
t_{2}
{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}
{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}
{\vec{p}}
-{\vec{p}}
{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{3}
{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}
time

Figure 3
:
Tree level pentaspectrum

Finally, we must also determined the tree-level six-point initial conditions, illustrated in
fig.

3
. We are specifically interested in the correlator

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{t}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle\,,

(4.44)

in the limit
p\gg k_{i}
, where
{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{t}\equiv\sum_{i=1}^{4}{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{i}
. As in the case of the trispectrum, we will isolate the term

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{t}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle_{\rm IC^{(2)}}\supset\lambda^{2}\Gamma_{2,4}(p)P_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{4})\,,

(4.45)

such that

\displaystyle\Gamma_{2,4}(p)

\displaystyle=\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4-2\alpha}}\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{4-2\alpha}}\Big\langle\phi^{2}(p,\tau_{1})\phi^{2}(p,\tau)\phi^{2}(p,\tau_{2})\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt-2\hskip 0.8pt{\rm Re}\int^{\tau}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{4-2\alpha}}\int^{\tau_{1}}\frac{{\rm d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{4-2\alpha}}\Big\langle\phi^{2}(p,\tau)\phi^{2}(p,\tau_{1})\phi^{2}(p,\tau_{2})\Big\rangle\,.

(4.46)

By direct calculation we find that

\displaystyle\Gamma_{2,4}(p)=\frac{1}{216p^{3+4\alpha}}\bigg[

\displaystyle 16+4\gamma_{E}(-11+3\gamma_{E})+3\pi^{2}+4(-11+6\gamma_{E}+3\log 2)\log 2

\displaystyle+{O}\bigg(\log\frac{p}{[aH]}\bigg)\bigg]\,.

(4.47)

5
Composite Operator Mixing

From our above discussion, we argued that corrections to Stochastic Inflation are uniquely determined by the correlation functions of composite operators; computing those that are relevant to correcting Stochastic Inflation up to NNLO is the topic of this section. We will start by setting up the problem of calculating composite operator renormalization. We are interested in operators of the form
\varphi_{+}^{n}({\vec{x}})
. These are well-defined purely within the EFT, so we must be able to discuss their correlation functions and renormalizations given only the EFT data. Of course, the crucial information is the initial conditions, which therefore depend on first matching to the UV.

In general, given the EFT field operator
\varphi_{+}({\vec{x}},\texttt{t})
, we can always define a composite operator

\varphi_{+}^{n}({\vec{x}})=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p_{i}}{(2\pi)^{3}}e^{-i{\vec{p}}_{i}\cdot{\vec{x}}}\varphi_{+}({\vec{p}}_{i})\,.

(5.1)

In a free theory, we find the structure:

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{n}\big({\vec{x}}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{n}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=n!\prod_{i=1}^{n}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p_{i}}{(2\pi)^{3}}e^{-i{\vec{p}}_{i}\cdot{\vec{x}}}(2\pi)^{3}\delta({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{i}+{\vec{p}}_{i})P_{+}(k_{i})

\displaystyle=n!\hskip 0.8pte^{i\sum\vec{k}_{i}\cdot{\vec{x}}}P_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{n})\,,

(5.2)

where
P_{+}
is defined in
eq.

4.37
.
Taking the Fourier transform,

\varphi_{+}^{n}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)=\int{\rm d}^{3}x\,e^{i{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364707}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364707}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\cdot{\vec{x}}}\varphi_{+}^{n}\big({\vec{x}}\big)\,,

(5.3)

we have

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{n}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}\hskip 0.8pt\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{n}\big)\Big\rangle=n!\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{n})\hskip 0.8pt\delta\Big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}+\sum{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{i}\Big)\,.

(5.4)

For simplicity, it will often be easiest to use
\varphi_{+}^{n}({\vec{x}}=0)\equiv\varphi_{+}^{n}[0]
to avoid the extra
\delta
-function.

Given this definition, we see that we should be able to define all such correlators of composite operators as simply integrals over the correlators of
\varphi_{+}
. For example,

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{n}\big[{\vec{x}}=0\big]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{m}\big)\Big\rangle=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p_{i}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{n}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{m}\big)\Big\rangle\,.

(5.5)

A very important feature of this formula is that the integrand on the RHS is free of divergences, in the sense that we should have already regulated and renormalized the expression. As a result, all of the renormalization of the composite operator itself (and hence the matrix of anomalous dimensions) has to be associated with integrals over
{\vec{p}}_{i}
as opposed to the loop integrals that appear in the calculation of
\big\langle\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{p}}_{n}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{m}\big)\big\rangle
itself.

5.1
One-loop Corrections

Trispectrum

We will start by computing
b_{1}
, which is determined from the four-point function via

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}\,.

(5.6)

The relationship between this loop contribution and the tree-level trispectrum is illustrated in
fig.

. The contribution to the four-point function from the time evolution already yields a
\log[aH]
. Therefore, any anomalous scaling (which should also only be a single log) must arise from the initial conditions. The relevant contribution was calculated in

.

Performing the integration over
p
using dynamical dim reg, we find

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\lambda\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt\times\frac{(1+2\alpha)\Gamma[-1+2\alpha]\sin\big(\frac{\pi}{2}(1-2\alpha)\big)}{2^{2\alpha}}\frac{K^{-2\alpha}\pi^{3/2}\Gamma[\alpha]}{\Gamma\big[\frac{3}{2}+\alpha\big]}\,.

(5.7)

Expanding for
\alpha\to 0
we have

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{2}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\lambda\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})\bigg(\frac{1}{48\pi^{2}\alpha^{2}}+\frac{(4-3\gamma_{E}-3\log K)}{72\pi^{2}\alpha}+{\rm finite}\bigg)

\displaystyle\to\frac{\lambda}{36\pi^{2}}\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})P_{+}(k_{2})(4-3\gamma_{E})\bigg(\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{[aH]}{k_{i}}\bigg)+\ldots\ .

(5.8)

We can repeat this calculation with
\varphi_{+}^{n}[0]
to determine the one-loop anomalous dimension for all
n
. Keeping track of combinatorics, one finds

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{n}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{n}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\supset\frac{\lambda}{36\pi^{2}}\binom{n}{2}n!\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.0pt\times\sum_{i}(4-3\gamma_{E})\bigg(\frac{1}{2\alpha}-\log\frac{[aH]}{k_{i}}+{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\right)+{\rm finite}\bigg)\,.

(5.9)

This NLO
\log[aH]
dependence can be resummed by including the following correction in the dynamical RG:

b_{1}=-\frac{\lambda}{36\pi^{2}}(4-3\gamma_{E})\,.

(5.10)

Six-point

Our next task is to compute
b_{2}
, which we determine by evaluating the six point function,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.0pt\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle=\int\!\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\bigg\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\bigg(\!\!-{\vec{p}}-\sum_{i=1}^{4}\vec{k}_{i}\bigg)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\bigg\rangle^{\prime}\,,

(5.11)

with
p\gg k_{i}
. The relationship between this one-loop contribution and the tree-level six-point function is illustrated in
fig.

5
. As before, only the initial conditions arising from

\Big\langle\varphi_{+}\big(\hskip 0.8pt{\vec{p}}\,\big)\varphi_{+}\big(-{\vec{p}}-{\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{t}\big)\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle^{\prime}\supset\lambda^{2}\hskip 0.8pt\Gamma_{2,4}(p)P_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{4})\,,

(5.12)

will contribute to the operator mixing, where
\Gamma_{2,4}(p)
was calculated in
eq.

4.47
.
Performing the momentum integration using dynamical dim reg, we find

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int\frac{{\rm d}^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\Gamma_{2,4}(p)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}\alpha}-\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}+\,...\,\right)\left[b_{2,4}+{O}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right]+{\rm finite}\,,

(5.13)

where

b_{2,4}=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{216}\Big[16+4\gamma_{E}(3\gamma_{E}-11)+3\pi^{2}+4\log 2(6\gamma_{E}+3\log 2-11)\Big]\ .

(5.14)

Now we restore the factor
(k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4})^{\alpha}[aH]^{-4\alpha}
associated with
P(k_{i})
for general
\alpha
and take the limit

to get

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{2}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{4}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\supset b_{2,4}P_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{4})\left[\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}\alpha}+\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{4}\frac{1}{4}\log\frac{k_{i}}{[aH]}\right]\,.

(5.15)

Repeating this calculation for
\varphi_{+}^{n}
we have

\displaystyle\Big\langle\varphi_{+}^{n}[0]\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{1}\big)\,...\,\varphi_{+}\big({\vec{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{364708}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{417140}{458752}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{511514}{458751}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}}}_{n+2}\big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\supset b_{2,4}\hskip 0.8pt\binom{n}{2}(n+2)!\hskip 0.8ptP_{+}(k_{1})\,...\,P_{+}(k_{n+2})

\displaystyle\hskip 22.0pt\times\left[\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}\alpha}+\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{4}\frac{1}{4}\log\frac{k_{i}}{[aH]}\right]\,.

(5.17)
